# Agents · T · part 18

887 entries, testskrivare to thinker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **testskrivare**: Skriver eller utökar tester i ett Android-repo (ReseApoteket, Dagboken, fonder) på rätt nivåer enligt repots testing-strategy, med repots delade testhjälpare, och bygger fixturer. Använd bara när en ändring berör tre eller fler testnivåer eller kräver många testfall (t.ex. en ny delad ram, en ny samling eller entitet hela vägen, beräkningskantfall, en fixtur med varje fält satt) – vanliga små ändringar testar huvudsessionen själv. ([Kodhast/claude-android](https://github.com/Kodhast/claude-android/blob/HEAD/plugins/android/agents/testskrivare.md))
- **testskrivare**: Skriver ett test utifrån ett acceptanskriterium eller ett steg i planen och visar att det är rött. Jest för API:t och pytest för den äldre prisberäkningen. Skriver ingen produktionskod. ([p4ker/ovningsprojekt-3](https://github.com/p4ker/ovningsprojekt-3/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/testskrivare.md))

Receives the same settled spec as the coder and writes requirements-facing tests without ever seeing the implementation; runs in parallel with the coder so the two independent interpretations of the spec can be compared. Proves its tests red on the base before claiming them. Netless: no network. - **tethys**: Tethys — pomona water-chemistry watchdog (Titaness of fresh water, mother of rivers: she keeps the waters in balance but never stirs them herself). Kết ĐẠT/TRẢ-VỀ-SỬA; CẤM phát hành khi còn lỗi đỏ. KHÔNG tạo nội dung mới — chỉ kiểm. - **theorem-assessor**: Assesses a candidate theorem list against the issues it is drawn from — the seed generator's list before a PR exists, or the theorems a review round's generator emitted — recommending per theorem accept, reject, change settle mode, merge, split or rewrite, and proposing a theorem for each acceptance criterion or stated behavior no theorem tests. It writes nothing on the branch and posts nothing. - **theorem-dependency-mapper**: Build a DAG of theorem/lemma/proposition dependencies across the paper. The operator describes the work; theRuler creates a stub track, runs bit:analyze's coordinator itself, dispatching one bit:analyst per topic for deep research, dispatches bit:scout on top of that research, re-runs analyze → scope until only the operator's own decisions remain open, returns one gate for the operator to approve the scope (looping back to a new analyze pass with any questions), then dispatches bit:planner and stops. Use when the caller wants the reasoning audit rather than the working.
